    > +   let l:filename = expand('%:t')      " current file name (no path)
    > +   let l:filepath = expand('%:p:h')    " current directory
    > + 
    > +   " Match trailing number (no magic mode for better compatibility)
    > +   if l:filename =~ '\(.*\.\)\([0-9]\+\)$'
    > +     let l:base   = substitute(l:filename, '\(.*\.\)\([0-9]\+\)$', '\1', '')
    > +     let l:number = substitute(l:filename, '\(.*\.\)\([0-9]\+\)$', '\2', '')
    > + 
    > +     let l:width  = strlen(l:number)
    > +     let l:newnum = str2nr(l:number) + 1
    > + 
    > +     " Preserve zero padding
    > +     let l:newnum_str = printf('%0*d', l:width, l:newnum)
    > + 
    > +     let l:newfile = l:filepath . '/' . l:base . l:newnum_str
    > + 
    > +     " Wipe current buffer, then open new file
    > +     execute 'bwipeout!'
    > +     execute 'edit' fnameescape(l:newfile)
    > +   else
    > +     echo "Filename does not end in a number."
    > +   endif
    > + endfunction
    > + 
    > + nnoremap <leader>n :call IncrementFilenameAndOpen()<CR>
